HISTORY AND DEVELOPMENT                                                       歷史沿革
1962    I Lan Foods Industrial Co., Ltd. (“I Lan Foods”) was established in   於1962年5月成立宜蘭食品工業股份有限公
        May 1962, which manufactured canned agricultural products mainly      司（「宜蘭食品」），該公司當時從事製造罐
        for export.                                                           頭農產品並以出口外銷業務為主。

1983    In 1983, we collaborated with Iwatsuka Confectionery Co., Ltd.        於1983年，我們與日本領先的米果製造商
        (“ICCL”), one of the leading Japanese rice cracker producers, to      之一的岩塚製菓株式會社（「岩塚製菓」）合
        jointly develop the rice cracker market in Taiwan region. Since       作，攜手開拓台灣地區米果市場。自1983
        1983, we have been producing and marketing our products under         年起，我們開始生產及營銷「旺旺」品牌的
        the “Want Want” brand.                                                產品。

1989    We introduced the “Want Want” brand in the Chinese Mainland in        1989年「旺旺」品牌進入中國境內市場。
        1989.

1992    We ventured into the Chinese Mainland and established our first       於1992年進軍中國境內並在湖南省設立首
        subsidiary in Hunan province in 1992. In the following years, we      間位於中國境內的附屬公司。此後數年，
        grew from a pure rice cracker company to a diversified food and       我們成功從一家單一米果公司發展為多元
        beverages company.                                                    化的食品及飲料公司。

1996    In May 1996, Want Want Holdings Ltd. (“WWHL”), our subsidiary,        我們的附屬公司旺旺控股有限公司（「旺旺
        was listed on the Main Board of Singapore Exchange Securities         控股」）於1996年5月在新加坡證券交易所有
        Trading Limited (“SGX-ST”) and subsequently diversified into the      限公司（「新交所」）主板上市。其後，更將
        hospital, hotel and property businesses and other investments.        業務作多元化發展至醫院、酒店及地產業
                                                                              務以及其他投資項目。

2007    WWHL delisted from SGX-ST in September 2007 and conducted              旺旺控股於2007年9月在新交所除牌。隨後
        a group restructuring which involved (i) the incorporation of Want     進行重組，包括(i)成立中國旺旺控股有限公
        Want China Holdings Limited (the “Company”) as the new holding         司（「本公司」）為其所有食品及飲料業務的
        company of our core operations related to the food and beverages       新投資控股公司；(ii)把醫院、酒店及地產
        businesses; (ii) the divestment and transfer of the hospital, hotel    業務以及其他投資項目剝離並於2007年12
        and property businesses and other investments to San Want              月31日轉移至San Want Holdings Limited
        Holdings Limited (“San Want”) on 31 December 2007, and the            （「神旺」），再透過向當時旺旺控股股東派
        interests in San Want were distributed to the then shareholders of     發神旺權益的實物股息而剝離。
        WWHL by way of dividend in specie.

2008    Our Company was listed on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong             於2008年3月26日，本公司正式在香港聯合
        Limited (the “HK Stock Exchange”) on 26 March 2008 (the “Listing”).   交易所有限公司（「香港聯交所」）上市（「上
                                                                              市」）。

2009-   Our Taiwan Depositary Receipts were listed on the Taiwan Stock        於2009年4月28日，我們的台灣存託憑證
2013    Exchange Corporation on 28 April 2009 and were voluntarily            在台灣證券交易所股份有限公司上市並於
        withdrawn from listing on 15 October 2013.                            2013年10月15日自願終止上市。

2020    In 2020, our Vietnam production base commenced construction           於2020年，越南生產基地開始動工，以
        to strengthen our business expansion and to cope with our future      強化集團在海外市場的業務拓展及未來佈
        development strategy in overseas markets.                             局。

2022    In 2022, our new factory in Tiền Giang province, Vietnam              於2022年，在越南前江省新設的工廠開始
        commenced production, representing the first step of Want Want        投入生產，這是我們旺旺為「世界夢」跨出
        towards the “Dream of the World”.                                     的第一步。

PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES                                                          主要業務
The principal activities of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively    本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「旺旺」、「我們」
referred to as “Want Want”, “We” or the “Group”) are the manufacturing,       或「集團」）之主要業務為米果、乳品及飲
distribution and sale of rice crackers, dairy products and beverages, snack   料、休閒食品及其他產品之製造、分銷及
foods and other products.                                                     銷售。

KEY MARKETS                                                                   主要市場
Most of our operations are located in the Chinese Mainland, which is one      我們的業務大部份位於中國境內（全球增長
of the fastest growing economies in the world. We have an extensive           速度最快經濟體之一）。我們在中國境內的
nationwide sales and distribution network throughout the Chinese Mainland.    全國性銷售及分銷網絡龐大，我們也出口
We also export our products to other markets, including North America, East   產品至其他市場：例如北美、東亞、東南
Asia, South East Asia and Europe.                                             亞及歐洲。

As at 31 March 2025, we had 419 sales offices, 35 production bases            於2025年3月31日，集團在中國境內有419
and 89 factories in the Chinese Mainland and worked with over 10,000          間營業所，35個生產基地及89間工廠，合
distributors.                                                                 作經銷商超過1萬名。

day's work and every slogan. Inheritance is the accumulation of Want            與每一句口號中。傳承，是旺旺人切身體
Want employees' personal experiences, while innovation is the fruit of their    驗的積累；創新，則是旺旺人敢於突破、
courage to break through barriers and scale new heights. Every corporate        勇攀高峰的果實。每一次企業文化活動，
culture activity serves as a driving force to rally employees' spirit and       都是凝聚員工精氣神、增強歸屬感的驅動
strengthen their sense of belonging, allowing each Want Want colleague to       力，讓每一位旺旺同仁都能感受到文化的
feel the warmth and power of the culture.                                       溫度與力量。

                                                                                                  Corporate Culture



Want Want Family Day                                                            旺旺家庭日
"Want Want Family Day" is a warm-hearted festival that originates from          「旺旺家庭日」是一個起於「緣」，歸於「家」
"fate" and returns to "home". Every Want Want Family Day is a beautiful         的溫情節日，每一次旺旺家庭日，都是旺
moment for Want Want people to meet and gather. Regardless of the               旺人相遇與相聚的美好時刻。無論春夏秋
season, happiness is defined here: In summer, everyone takes a bite of          冬，快樂在這裡被定義：夏日裡，大家吃
Want Want popsicle, frolics in the water, checks out the food stalls all over   一口旺冰，撒歡玩水，打卡滿場美食攤
the venue, and makes creative handicrafts with their own hands; while in        位，動手製作創意手工；冬日裡，捧一杯
winter, they hold a cup of Want Want beverage, taste Want Want's creative       旺飲，品嘗旺旺創意美食，逛逛專屬旺旺
snacks, stroll through the exclusive "Want Want Bazaar" for Want Want           人 的「旺 市 集」， 溫 暖 而 不 膩 ， 幸 福 又 美
people, feeling warm, happy and content but not cloying.                        滿。

The event not only enhances the cohesion and sense of belonging among           活動不僅增進了員工的凝聚力與歸屬感，
the employees but also conveys the cultural spirit of Want Want people to       更傳遞了旺旺人「有心、用心」做好每件事
"do everything with heart and dedication". Through the Family Day, more         的文化精神。通過家庭日，讓更多人瞭解
people get to know Want Want, recognize Want Want, and fall in love with        旺旺、認識旺旺、愛上旺旺，也讓旺旺成
Want Want, while Want Want also becomes a strong backing for every              為每一個幸福小家庭背後的堅強後盾。未
happy small family. In the future, let's continue to move forward hand in       來讓我們繼續攜手前行，讓「旺旺一家人」
hand, so that the vision of "Want Want Family" can shine in every corner!       的願景在每一個角落綻放光彩！

旺旺精神
Spirit of Want Want




                      經營理念：                              MANAGEMENT PHILOSOPHY:
                      緣 頭圓握髮                             People Oriented

                      旺仔的頭形是圓，代表「投緣」。頭部                  Hot-Kid’s round head shape signifies congeniality. The head
                      好像剛洗完髮，髮未乾，故有握髮之                   seems to have just been washed but the hair is not dry, so it is
                      稱。「握髮」出自《周公誡子》中，「一沐                known as “hair holding”. “Hair holding” originated from <Zhou Gong
                      三握髮，一飯三吐哺，猶恐失天下之                   Quotation>: “three times hair-holding during one shower and three
                      士」。所以握髮也表示有延攬賢士的意                  times spitting the food out during one meal with the fear of losing
                      思。                                 the talented people in the world”. So, “Hair holding” also have the
                                                         meaning of eagerness to recruit talents.

                      董事長的話：公司非常惜緣惜才，公                   Message from our Chairman: our Company is very grateful for and
                      司就像一個大家庭，大家有緣在一                    cherishes our talents. Our Company is like a big family: we should
                      起，應該盡我們的職責，珍惜這個家                   cherish the fate that brought us together, play our part and cherish
                      庭，亦即你心中有我，我心中有你，                   this family, and put one another in our own hearts.
                      這才是真正惜緣。




  自信     笑口由己         誠心            Self Conﬁdence

  旺仔笑口常開代表充滿自信。你的自                  Confidence comes with good preparation. Hot-Kid’s smiling
  信來自你周全的準備，當你充滿自信                  countenance reflects our belief in preparedness and the confidence
  時，一定可以充滿笑容。自信是我們                  that glows with it. The heart-shaped tongue of Hot-Kid represents
  做事的第一基礎。笑口中舌如心形，                  our sincere heart to the Group: Be faithful to the leaders, be kind
  代表著我們赤誠的心：對待上級全心                  to the subordinates, and be sincere to our customers. This effort
  服從愛戴，對待下屬關心倍至，對待                  would definitely enhance our performance.

14     2024/2025年報           ANNUAL REPORT

主要獎項及榮譽
Major Awards and Recognition



旺旺集團榮獲首家零碳工廠證書
旺旺集團旗下工廠嘉興美旺機械製造有限公司獲頒「零碳工廠評價證書」，成為旺旺集團旗下首家
獲得零碳認證的工廠，助力實現「雙碳」目標。其後，瀋陽總廠榮獲「零碳」工廠建設典型案例。

Want Want Group Awarded the First Zero-Carbon Factory Certificate
Jiaxing Mei-Want Machinery Ltd., a factory under the Want Want Group, has been awarded the
"Zero-Carbon Factory Evaluation Certificate", becoming the first factory under the Want Want
Group to obtain zero-carbon factory certification and helping achieve the "dual carbon" goals.
Subsequently, the Shenyang General Factory was honored as a model of the construction of a
"zero-carbon" factory.



旺旺集團綠色製造再添佳績，新增三家工廠榮獲國家級綠色工廠認證
2024年國家工業和信息化部公示了《2024年度綠色製造名單》，旺旺集團旗下湖南旺旺、廣州永旺
及廣西明旺三家工廠順利通過國家級綠色工廠認證。至此，旺旺集團已擁有10家國家級綠色工廠。

Want Want Group Making Further Achievements in Green Manufacturing, with an
addition of Three Factories Awarded National Certification for Green Factories
In 2024, the Ministry of Industry and Information Technology of China publicized the 2024 Green
Manufacturing List. Three factories of Want Want Group – Hunan Want Want, Guangzhou Yong-
Want, and Guangxi Ming-Want – successfully passed the national certification for green factories.
So far, Want Want Group has 10 national-level green factories.




旺旺集團榮獲優秀休閒食品創新製造企業榮譽稱號
中國食品工業協會馬鈴薯食品專業委員會年會、第十八屆全國休閒食品產業峰會暨莊浪
招商引資大會在莊浪縣舉行。旺旺榮獲中國食品工業協會馬鈴薯食品專業委員會2023年
度優秀休閒食品創新製造企業榮譽稱號。

Want Want Group Awarded the Honorary Title of Outstanding Innovative
Manufacturing Enterprise in Snack Food Industry
The Annual Meeting of the Potato Food Professional Committee of the China National Food Industry Association, the 18th
National Snack Food Industry Summit and Zhuanglang Investment Promotion Conference were held in Zhuanglang County.
Want Want was awarded the honorary title of "2023 Outstanding Innovative Manufacturing Enterprise in Snack Food
Industry" by the Potato Food Professional Committee of the China National Food Industry Association.




旺旺集團榮獲中國方便食品行業創新產品獎
由中國食品科學技術學會在北京舉辦的第二十四屆中國方便食品大會上，旺旺Q米面（酸湯肥牛
味）和哎呦麻糬濃湯（蘑菇、玉米、羅宋）榮獲2023-2024年度方便食品行業創新產品獎。

Want Want Group Winning Innovation Product Award in China's Convenience
Food Industry
At the 24th China Convenience Food Conference hosted by the Chinese Institute of Food
Science and Technology in Beijing, Want Want Q Rice Noodles (Sour and Spicy Beef Soup
Flavour) and Aiyou Mochi Soup (Mushroom, Corn, and Borscht Flavours) were awarded the
2023-2024 Innovation Product Award in the convenience food industry.

旺旺集團榮獲中國乳製品工業協會兩項殊榮
旺旺獲選為2024年中國乳業科技創新企業、其「一種無菌後均質的實現方式及其應用」
榮獲技術進步獎二等獎。

Want Want Group Winning Two Awards from China Dairy Industry
Association
Want Want was recognised as the "2024 China Dairy Industry Science and
Technology Innovation Enterprise", and its "Method for Implementation and
Application of Post-aseptic Homogenisation Technology" won the Second Prize of
the Technology Advancement Award.


旺旺集團榮獲iSEE全球獎等多項殊榮
旺旺集團榮獲iSEE全球獎多項殊榮，集團旺仔兒童堅果牛奶榮登
「iSEE創新品牌百強榜」， 每日喝牛奶榮登「iSEE創新技術TOP榜」，
旺旺豌豆酥榮獲「iSEE全球美味獎」三星，旺仔牛奶糖冰淇淋系列、
旺旺小小運動飲料榮獲「iSEE全球美味獎」二星，旺旺仙草凍、Fix
XBody燕麥奶榮獲「iSEE全球美味獎」一星殊榮，邦德薄荷咖啡榮獲
「iSEE創新品牌百強」，旺旺邦德超咖啡榮獲「全球食品創新獎」銀
獎，旺旺集團蔡旺庭總經理榮獲「年度創新人物」。

Want Want Group Winning Multiple iSEE Global Awards
Want Want Group has won multiple honours at the iSEE Global
Awards. The Group’s “Hot-Kid Nut Milk for Kids” (旺仔兒童堅果牛奶)
was included in the “iSEE Top 100 Innovative Brands List”, while its
“Daily Milk” (每日喝牛奶) was included in the “iSEE Top Innovative Technologies List”. “Want Want Pea Crisps” (旺旺豌豆
酥) won three stars in the “iSEE Global Taste Award”, “Hot-Kid Milk Chewies Ice Cream Series” (旺仔牛奶糖冰淇淋系列) and
“Want Want Mini Sports Drink” (旺旺小小運動飲料) received two stars in the “iSEE Global Taste Award”, and “Want Want
Xiancao Jelly” (旺旺仙草凍) and “Fix XBody Oat Milk” (Fix XBody燕麥奶) were awarded one star in the “iSEE Global Taste
Award”. “Mr. Bond Mint Coffee” (邦德薄荷咖啡) was listed as one of the “iSEE Top 100 Innovative Brands”, “Want Want Mr.
Bond Coffee Rush” (旺旺邦德超咖啡) won the Silver Award of the “Global Food Innovation Award”, and Mr. Tsai Wang-Ting,
the General Manager of Want Want Group, was honoured as the “Innovator of the Year”.


旺旺集團獲選為2024年台灣25大國際品牌
旺旺集團連年入榜「台灣25大國際品牌」，是對旺旺金字招牌和優質口碑的肯定。

Want Want Group was recognised as the Top 25 Best Taiwan Global Brands in 2024
Want Want Group has been listed as one of the “Top 25 Best Taiwan Global Brands” for years,
which is a recognition of Want Want’s prestigious and high-quality brand reputation.

of the Group.                                                                      給本集團的最終消費者。

Board Proceedings                                                                  董事會議事程序
Regular Board meetings for each year are generally scheduled in advance            每年董事會定期會議的舉行日期一般都會
to give Directors adequate time to plan their schedules to attend the              預先編定，使各董事有充裕時間安排出席
meetings. Notices of regular Board meetings are served to all Directors at         會議。董事會定期會議通告於會議至少14
least 14 days before the meetings. For other Board meetings, reasonable            天前送交全體董事。至於召開其他董事會
notice would be given.                                                             會議，將發出合理通知。

One of the important roles of the Chairman is to lead the Board to ensure          主席其中一項重要職責是領導董事會確保
that the Board operates effectively and fully performs its responsibilities. All   董事會有效地運作及充分履行其應有職
Directors are encouraged to actively participate in the discussion at Board        責。主席鼓勵所有董事積極參與討論，並
meetings, and the Chairman has allowed sufficient time for discussion of           給予充分時間討論，以確保董事會的決定
issues to ensure that Board decisions fairly reflect the consensus of the          能公正反映董事會的共識。
Board.

CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                             主席及行政總裁
Code provision C.2.1 provides that the roles of chairman and chief executive     根據守則條文第C.2.1條，主席與行政總裁
should be separate and should not be performed by the same person.               的角色應予區分，不應由一人同時兼任。
Our Company deviates from this provision because Mr. Tsai Eng-Meng               由於蔡衍明先生擔任主席兼行政總裁兩個
performs both the roles of chairman and chief executive. Mr. Tsai is the         職務，故本公司偏離此條文。蔡先生為本
founder of our Group and has over 48 years of experience in the food and         集團之創辦人，於食品及飲料行業方面擁
beverages industry. Given the current stage of development of our Group,         有逾48年經驗。考慮到本集團目前之發展
the Board believes that vesting the two roles in the same person provides        階段，董事會認為，由一人同時兼任主席
our Company with strong and consistent leadership and facilitates the            與行政總裁，在實施並執行本集團的業務
implementation and execution of our Group’s business strategies. We shall        策略時，可為本公司提供強大而貫徹之領
nevertheless review the structure from time to time in light of the prevailing   導。然而，本集團將根據屆時情況不時檢
circumstances.                                                                   討現行架構。

Method for convening an extraordinary general meeting                            召開股東特別大會的方法
Any two or more shareholders, or any one shareholder which is a                  根據公司章程細則列明之規定及程序，兩

                                                                                       ）持有本公司不少於十分之一
Company, make a requisition to the Board to convene an extraordinary             繳足股本之股東，可根據公司章程細則向
general meeting of the Company and put forward proposals at the meeting.         本公司董事會要求召開股東特別大會，並
The objects of the meeting must be stated in the written requisition which       於會上提呈議案。召開會議之目的必須列

ENVIRONMENTAL PROTECTION                                                           環境保護
The Group focuses not only on manufacturing but also on reciprocating the          本集團堅持把可持續發展和善盡社會責任
communities by making sustainable development and social responsibility            作為發展的重要工作。在關注生產的同
contributions as important parts of the Group’s development. The Group             時，不忘回饋社會，亦致力保護其營運的
endeavors to protect the environment in which it operates its business, so as      環境，有助實現可持續發展及經營。
to realise sustainable development and operation.

The Group encourages environmental protection, advocates energy                    本集團鼓勵環保，並在本集團內部倡導節
conservation and emission reduction within the Group, emphasises the               能減排、保護資源與環境的重要性，以及
importance of protecting natural resources and environment and promotes            提升員工的環保意識，讓履行環保與社會
environmental awareness among its employees, making the fulfillment of             責任成為集團每一位管理者和員工共同的
environmental and social responsibilities the common value pursuit as well         價值取向及工作職責的一部分。
as job responsibilities of every member of the management and every staff
member.

We are committed to promoting sustainable development, with green factory          我們致力推進可持續發展管理方針，以綠
declaration, green energy development, significant improvement in energy           色工廠申報、綠色能源發展、能源效率大

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                                  股息政策
The Company has adopted a dividend policy (the “Dividend Policy”). It is the     本公司已採納股息政策（「股息政策」）。作為
policy of the Company that, in recommending or declaring dividends, the          本公司一貫的政策，在建議或宣佈股息時，
Board shall make distributions to allow its shareholders to participate in the   本公司董事會應進行派發以允許股東分享其
Company’s profits whilst retaining adequate cash reserves for meeting its        利潤同時維持充足的現金儲備以滿足其日常
requirements for working capital and long-term sustainable development.          營運資金及長期可持續發展的要求。

The Board has the discretion to propose, declare and distribute dividends        在不違反公司章程細則及一切適用法律與
to the shareholders of the Company, subject to the Articles of Association       法規的前提下、並經考慮以下列明的因
of the Company and all applicable laws and regulations and taking into           素，董事會有酌情權向本公司股東提議、
account the factors set out below.                                               宣佈及派發股息。

The Board shall take into account the following factors of the Group when        董事會在考慮進行宣佈及支付股息時應計
considering the declaration and payment of dividends:                            及集團的以下因素：

(i)     the financial performance and cash flow situation of the Group;          (i)     本集團之財務表現及現金流狀況；

(ii)    the Group’s business strategies and operations, including future         (ii)    本集團之業務策略及營運，包括未來
        capital requirements and investment needs;                                       對資金需求及投資需要；

(iii)   economic conditions and other internal or external factors that may      (iii)   可能對本集團業務或財務表現及狀況
        have an impact on the business or financial performance and position             產生影響之經濟狀況及其他內部或外
        of the Group;                                                                    部因素；

(iv)    amount of share repurchases made during the period; and                  (iv)    期內進行的股份回購的金額；及

(v)     any other factors that the Board may consider relevant.                  (v)     其他董事會認為相關的因素。

The Company in general meeting may from time to time declare dividends           本公司股東大會可不時向其成員宣佈股
to be paid to the members but no dividends shall be declared in excess of        息，惟其金額不可多於董事會建議的金
the amount recommended by the Board.                                             額。

In addition, the Directors may from time to time pay such interim dividends      除此之外，董事可不時以其認為適宜的金
or special dividends of such amounts and on such dates as they think fit.        額及日期支付中期股息，或特別股息。

The Company may declare and pay dividends by way of cash or warrant or           在不違反公司章程細則的前提下，本公司
by other means that the Board considers appropriate, subject to its Articles     可宣佈及支付現金或認股權證或董事會認
of Association.                                                                  為適宜的其他形式的股息。

going concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going concern       況下披露與持續經營有關的事項，以及使
and using the going concern basis of accounting unless the directors of the      用持續經營為會計基礎，除非貴公司董事
Company either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease operations or have      有意將貴集團清盤或停止經營，或別無其
no realistic alternative but to do so.                                           他實際的替代方案。

The directors of the Company are assisted by the Audit Committee in              審核委員會協助貴公司董事履行職責，監

reporting process.

     manufacture and distribution of food and beverages. The Group’s                      製造及分銷食品和飲料。本集團的活
     activities are primarily conducted in the People’s Republic of China                 動主要在中華人民共和國（「中國」）進
     (“the PRC”) and its products are also sold to North America, East Asia,              行，其產品亦銷往北美、東亞、東南
     Southeast Asia and Europe.                                                           亞及歐洲。

     The Company was incorporated in the Cayman Islands on 3 October                      本公司於2007年10月3日根據開曼群
     2007 as an exempted company with limited liability under the                         島公司法在開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁
     Companies Law of the Cayman Islands. The address of its registered                   免有限公司。本公司的註冊辦事處
     office is Maples Corporate Services Limited, P.O. Box 309, Ugland                    地址為Maples Corporate Services

      2.4   Subsidiaries                                                              2.4   附屬公司

      2.4.1 Consolidation                                                             2.4.1 合併賬目

      A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity) over which the        附屬公司指本集團擁有控制權的所有
      Group has control. The Group controls an entity where the Group is              主體（包括結構化主體）。當本集團因
      exposed to, or has rights, to variable returns from its involvement with        參與該主體的營運而承擔可變回報的
      the entity and has the ability to affect those returns through its power        風險或享有可變回報的權益並有能力
      over the entity. Subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on which           透過其對該主體的權力影響此等回報
      control is transferred to the Group except for business combination             時，本集團即控制該主體。附屬公司
      under common control as described below. They are deconsolidated                在控制權轉移至本集團之日起合併
      from the date that control ceases.                                              入賬（除下述同一控制下企業合併以
                                                                                      外）。附屬公司在控制權終止之日起
                                                                                      停止合併入賬。

      Inter-company transactions, balances, income and expenses on                    集團內公司之間的交易、交易的結
      transactions between group companies are eliminated. Profits and                餘、收入及開支予以對銷。來自集團
      losses resulting from inter-company transactions that are recognised            內公司間的利潤和損失（確認於資產）
      in assets are also eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence          亦予以對銷，除非交易提供轉讓資產
      of an impairment of the transferred asset. Accounting policies                  減值證據則另作別論。附屬公司的會
      of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                     計政策已按需要作出改變，以確保與
      consistency with the policies adopted by the Group.                             本集團採用的政策保持一致。

      (a)   Business combinations                                                     (a)   業務合併

      (i)   Merger accounting for business combination under common                   (i)   同一控制下企業合併的合併法
            control

      The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial                 綜合財務報表中納入同一控制下企業
      statements of the combining entities or businesses in business                  合併中的合併實體或業務的財務報
      combinations under common control as if they had been combined                  表，視同該合併實體或業務自最終控
      from the date when the combining entities or businesses first came              股股東開始實施控制時一直是合併體
      under the control of the ultimate controlling shareholder.                      系。

      The net assets of the combining entities or businesses are                      合併實體或業務的資產淨值從最終控
      consolidated using the carrying amount from the ultimate controlling            股股東的角度以賬面值合併。鑒於最
      shareholder’s perspective. No amount is recognised for goodwill or the          終控股股東的利益的延續，在同一控
      excess of the Group’s interest in the book value of the net assets over         制下企業合併時，商譽或本集團對淨
      cost at the time of the common control combination, to the extent of            資產賬面值超出成本部分的權益，不
      the continuation of the ultimate controlling shareholder’s interest.            予以確認。

     owners of the acquiree and the equity interests issued by the Group.                 股權的公平值。轉讓對價包括或有對
     The consideration transferred includes the fair value of any asset                   價安排產生的任何資產或負債的公平
     or liability resulting from a contingent consideration arrangement.                  值。於業務合併時所購買的可辨認資
     Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent liabilities              產及所承擔的負債及或然負債，初步

     values at the acquisition date.

     Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                                  購買相關成本於產生時列為開支。

     Goodwill is initially measured as the excess of the aggregate of the                 商譽初步按所轉撥總對價及所收購非
     consideration transferred and the fair value of non-controlling interest             控制性權益之公平值超出所收購可辨
     over the net identifiable assets acquired and liabilities assumed. If                認資產淨值及所承擔負債之數額計
     this consideration is lower than the fair value of the net assets of the             量。倘此對價低於所購買附屬公司資
     subsidiary acquired, the difference is recognised in profit or loss.                 產淨值之公平值，則差額於損益中確
                                                                                          認。

      2.5   Associates                                                                    2.5   聯營公司

      An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant                      聯營公司為本集團對其有重大影響力
      influence but not control, generally accompanying a shareholding of                 而無控制權的實體，通常附帶有20%
      between 20% and 50% of the voting rights. Investments in associates                 至50%投票權的股權。聯營公司投
      are accounted for using the equity method of accounting. Under the                  資以權益法入賬。根據權益法，投資
      equity method, the investment is initially recognised at cost, and the              初步以成本確認，賬面值會增加或減
      carrying amount is increased or decreased to recognise the investor’s               少，以確認投資者佔被投資方收購日
      share of the profit or loss of the investee after the date of acquisition.          期後損益之比例。於收購於聯營公司
      Upon the acquisition of the ownership interest in an associate, any                 之擁有權權益時，聯營公司之成本與
      difference between the cost of the associate and the Group’s share of               本集團應佔聯營公司之可辨認資產及
      the net fair value of the associate’s identifiable assets and liabilities is        負債之公平淨值之任何差額入賬列作
      accounted for as goodwill.                                                          商譽。

      If the ownership interest in an associate is reduced but significant                如聯營公司的權益持有被削減但仍保
      influence is retained, only a proportionate share of the amounts                    留重大影響力，只有按比例將之前在
      previously recognised in other comprehensive income is reclassified                 其他全面收益中確認的數額重新分類
      to profit or loss where appropriate.                                                至損益（如適用）。

      2.7   Foreign currency translation                                               2.7   外幣折算

      (a)   Functional and presentation currency                                       (a)   功能和列報貨幣

      Items included in the financial statements of each of the Group’s                本集團每個主體的財務報表所列項目
      entities are measured using the currency of the primary economic                 均以該主體經營所在的主要經濟環境
      environment in which the entity operates (“the functional currency”).            的貨幣（「功能貨幣」）計量。本公司的
      The Company’s functional currency is United States Dollar (“US$”).               功能貨幣為美元（「美元」）。本集團
      The Group’s principal subsidiaries were incorporated in the PRC and              的主要附屬公司於中國註冊成立及該
      these subsidiaries considered RMB as their functional currency. Since            等附屬公司視人民幣為其功能貨幣。
      the majority of the assets and operations of the Group are located in            由於本集團大部分資產及業務位於中
      the PRC, the consolidated financial statements are presented in RMB              國，故綜合財務報表以人民幣呈報
      (unless otherwise stated) to reflect the primary economic environment            （除另有說明外）以反映本集團業務所在
      of the Group’s operations.                                                       主要經濟環境。

      transactions or valuation where items are remeasured. Foreign                    算此等交易產生的匯兌收益和虧損以

      transactions and from the translation at year-end exchange rates of              終匯率折算產生的匯兌收益和虧損在
      monetary assets and liabilities denominated in foreign currencies are            綜合收益表確認。
      recognised in the consolidated income statement.

      All foreign exchange gains and losses are presented in the                       所有匯兌收益和虧損在綜合收益表內
      consolidated income statement within “other gains – net”.                        的「其他收益－淨額」中列報。

      Translation differences on non-monetary financial assets and liabilities         非貨幣性金融資產及負債（例如按公
      such as equities held at fair value through profit or loss are recognised        平值透過損益持有的權益）的折算差
      in profit or loss as part of the fair value gain or loss. Translation            額在損益中確認為公平值收益和虧損

      2.8   Property, plant and equipment                                              2.8   物業、廠房及設備

      Property, plant and equipment are stated at historical cost less                 物業、廠房及設備乃按歷史成本值減
      accumulated depreciation and accumulated impairment losses, if any.              累計折舊及累計減值虧損（如有）後列
      Historical cost includes expenditure that is directly attributable to the        賬。歷史成本包括收購該等項目直接
      acquisition of the items.                                                        產生的開支。

      Freehold land is stated at cost less accumulated impairment losses, if           永久業權土地按成本減累計減值虧損
      any. Cost represents consideration paid for the purchase of the land.            （如有）後列賬。成本指購買土地已付
      Freehold land is not subject to depreciation.                                    對價。永久業權土地不計提折舊。

      Construction in progress (the “CIP”) represents buildings, plant and             在建工程（「在建工程」）代表在建或有
      machinery under construction or pending installation and is stated               待安裝的樓宇、廠房及機器，以成本
      at cost less accumulated impairment losses, if any. Cost includes                減累計減值虧損（如有）列賬。成本包

     2.13.4 Impairment                                                                      2.13.4 減值

     The Group assesses on a forward-looking basis the expected                             本集團按前瞻性基準評估其按攤銷成
     credit losses (“ECLs”) associated with its debt instruments carried                    本、按公平值透過損益記賬及按公平
     at amortised cost, FVPL and FVOCI. The Group measures a loss                           值透過其他全面收益記賬的債務工具
     allowance for a financial asset at an amount equal to the lifetime ECL                 的相關預期信用損失（「預期信用損
     if the credit risk on that financial asset has increased significantly                 失」）。如果該金融資產的信用風險自
     since initial recognition. If the credit risk on a financial asset has not             初始確認後大幅增加，則本集團計量
     increased significantly since initial recognition, the Group measures                  金融資產的虧損撥備，其金額等於整
     the loss allowance for that financial asset at an amount equal to the                  個存續期的預期信用損失。如果金融
     12-month ECL.                                                                          資產的信用風險自初始確認後未顯著
                                                                                            增加，則本集團以等於12個月預期信
                                                                                            用損失的金額計量該金融資產的虧損
                                                                                            撥備。

     For trade receivables, the Group applies the simplified approach                       就貿易應收款而言，本集團採用香港
     permitted by HKFRS 9, which requires expected lifetime losses to be                    財務報告準則第9號所准許的簡化方
     recognised from initial recognition of the receivables, see Note 3.1(b)                法，該方法規定於初步確認應收款時
     for further details.                                                                   須確認預期使用年期虧損。有關進一
                                                                                            步詳情請參閱附註3.1(b)。

     2.14 Inventories                                                                       2.14 存貨

     finished goods and work in progress comprises raw materials, labour,                   製成品及在製品的成本包括原材料、

     operating capacity). It excludes borrowing costs. Net realisable value                 產費用（依據正常經營能力）。這不包
     is the estimated selling price in the ordinary course of business, less                括借款費用。可變現淨值為在日常營
     applicable variable selling costs.                                                     運活動中的估計銷售價，減適用的變
                                                                                            動出售成本。

     2.18 Financial liabilities                                                              2.18 金融負債

     2.18.1 Classification                                                                   2.18.1 分類

     The Group’s financial liabilities include trade payables, accruals and                  本集團的金融負債包括貿易應付款、
     other payables, lease liabilities, borrowings and other non-current                     應計費用及其他應付款、租賃負債、
     liabilities.                                                                            借款及其他非流動負債。

     2.18.2 Recognition and derecognition                                                    2.18.2 確認及終止確認

     Financial liabilities are recognised when and only when the Group                       當且僅當本集團成為工具合約條款的
     becomes a party to the contractual provisions of the instruments and                    訂約方及於交易日，金融負債予以確
     on a trade date basis. A financial liability is derecognised when and                   認。當且僅當責任消除時，即相關合
     only when the liability is extinguished, that is, when the obligation                   同中規定的義務被解除或取消，或到
     specified in the relevant contract is discharged or cancelled, or                       期時，金融負債才終止確認。
     expires.

     2.18.3 Measurement                                                                      2.18.3 計量

     All financial liabilities are recognised initially at their fair value and              所有金融負債首次按其公平值確認，
     subsequently measured at amortised cost, using the effective interest                   及其後以實際利率法按攤銷成本計
     method, unless the effect of discounting would be insignificant, in                     量，惟倘折現之影響並不重大，則按
     which case they are stated at cost.                                                     成本列賬。

     Trade payables are recognised initially at fair value and subsequently                  貿易應付款初始以公平值確認，其後
     measured at amortised cost using the effective interest method.                         利用實際利率法按攤銷成本計量。

     2.19 Borrowings                                                                         2.19 借款

     Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction                   借款初始按公平值並扣除產生的交易
     costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently stated at amortised cost;                   費用確認。借款其後按攤銷成本列
     any difference between the proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the                  賬；所得款（扣除交易成本）與贖回價
     redemption value is recognised in the consolidated income statement                     值的任何差額利用實際利率法於借款
     over the period of the borrowings using the effective interest method.                  期間內在綜合收益表確認。

     Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are recognised as                     設立貸款融資時支付的費用倘部分或
     transaction costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some               全部融資將會很可能提取，該費用確
     or all of the facility will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred            認為貸款的交易費用。在此情況下，
     until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence that it                  費用遞延至貸款提取為止。如沒有證
     is probable that some or all of the facility will be drawn down, the fee is             據證明部分或全部融資將會很可能被
     capitalised as a prepayment for liquidity services and amortised over                   提取，則該費用資本化作為流動資金
     the period of the facility to which it relates.                                         服務的預付款，並按有關的融資期間
                                                                                             攤銷。

     Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the Group has                   除非本集團可無條件將負債的結算遞
     an unconditional right to defer settlement of the liability for at least 12             延至資產負債表日後最少12個月，否則
     months after the balance sheet date.                                                    借款分類為流動負債。

     rates on government bonds are used.

     The current service cost of the defined benefit plan, recognised in                  界定受益計劃的當期服務成本於綜合
     the consolidated income statement in employee benefit expense,                       收益表確認為員工福利開支（已包括
     except where included in the cost of an asset, reflects the increase                 在資產成本內除外），反映在現年度
     in the defined benefit obligation resulting from employee service in                 因為員工服務而產生的界定福利債務
     the current year. Changes in the present value of the defined benefit                增加。因計劃修訂或削減而導致的界
     obligation resulting from plan amendments or curtailments are                        定受益義務的現值變動，會立即在損
     recognised immediately in profit or loss as past service costs.                      益中確認為過往服務成本。

     Past-service costs are recognised immediately in income.                             過往服務成本即時於收益內確認。

     The net interest cost is calculated by applying the discount rate to                 淨利息成本採用界定受益責任的淨結
     the net balance of the defined benefit obligation and the fair value of              餘之貼現率及計劃資產的公平值計
     plan assets. This cost is included in employee benefit expense in the                算。此項成本列入綜合收益表的員工
     consolidated income statement.                                                       福利開支內。

     Remeasurement and actuarial gains and losses arising from                            因按經驗作出調整及精算假設改變而
     experience adjustments and changes in actuarial assumptions                          產生的精算盈虧在其發生期間，直接
     are recognised in the period in which they occur, directly in other                  在其他全面收益中確認。它們包括在
     comprehensive income. They are included in capital reserves in the                   權益變動表及綜合資產負債表的資本

      2.23 Provisions                                                                   2.23 準備

      Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present legal or                   當本集團因已發生的事件而產生現有
      constructive obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that an        的法律或推定義務；很可能需要有資
      outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation; and the           源的流出以結算義務；及金額已被可
      amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are not recognised for             靠估計，則確認準備。但不會就未來
      future operating losses.                                                          經營虧損確認準備。

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an           如有多項類似義務，其需要在結算中
      outflow will be required in settlement is determined by considering the           有資源流出的可能性，則可根據義務
      class of obligations as a whole. A provision is recognised even if the            的類別整體考慮。即使在同一義務類
      likelihood of an outflow with respect to any one item included in the             別所包含的任何一個項目相關的資源
      same class of obligations may be small.                                           流出的可能性極低，仍須確認準備。

      Provisions are measured at the present value of the expenditures                  準備採用稅前利率按照預期需結算有
      expected to be required to settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate             關義務的支出現值計量，該利率反映
      that reflects current market assessments of the time value of money               當時市場對金錢時間值和有關義務固
      and the risks specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision           有風險的評估。隨著時間過去而增加
      due to passage of time is recognised as interest expense.                         的準備確認為利息費用。

      discretion over the channel and price to sell the products, and there is          渠道及售價及並無可能影響客戶接納
      no unfulfilled obligation that could affect the customer’s acceptance of          產品的未履行責任時確認。當產品運
      the products. Delivery occurs when the products have been shipped                 送到指定地點時交付即告完成。當客
      to the specific location, the risks of obsolescence and loss have been            戶按照銷售合約接納產品，或接納條

      2.27 Leases                                                                        2.27 租賃

      impose any covenants, and leased assets may not be used as security                施加任何條款，而租賃資產不得用作
      for borrowing purposes.                                                            借款的擔保品。

      Each lease payment is allocated between the liability cost and finance             每筆租賃付款分配至負債成本及融資
      cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or loss over the lease period          成本。融資成本於租期內自損益扣
      so as to produce a constant periodic rate of interest on the remaining             除，藉此制定各期間負債結餘的固定
      balance of the liability for each period. The right-of-use assets are              週期利率。使用權資產按資產可使用
      depreciated over the shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease              年期或租期（以較短者為準）以直線法
      term on a straight-line basis. If the Group is reasonably certain to               折舊。倘本集團合理確定行使購買選
      exercise a purchase option, the right-of-use asset is depreciated over             擇權，則使用權資產於相關資產的可
      the underlying asset’s useful life.                                                使用年期內予以折舊。

     The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in              租賃付款額按照租賃內含利率貼現。
     the lease. If that rate cannot be determined, the lessee’s incremental             如果無法確定該利率，則應採用承租
     borrowing rate is used, being the rate that the lessee would have to               人的增量借款利率，即承租人為在類
     pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of similar value              似經濟環境下獲得價值相近的資產，
     in a similar economic environment with similar terms and conditions.               以類似條款及條件借入資金而必須支
                                                                                        付的利率。

     To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group uses recent                 為釐定增量借款利率，本集團以個別
     third-party financing received by the individual lessee as a starting              承租人最近收取的第三方融資作為起
     point, adjusted to reflect changes in financing condition since third              點，並調整以反映自收取第三方融資
     party financing was received.                                                      以來的融資狀況變動。

     All land in the PRC is stated-owned and no individual land ownership               所有於中國的土地均屬國家擁有，故
     right exists. The Group acquired the right to use certain land.                    並無個人土地擁有權。本集團取得使
     The premiums paid for such right are treated as prepayments for                    用若干土地的權利。就有關權利支付
     operating lease and recorded at cost as right-of-use assets, which are             的地價視為經營租賃的預付款項，並
     depreciated over the lease periods using the straight-line method.                 按成本入賬為使用權資產，於租賃期
                                                                                        內以直綫法折舊。

          less any lease incentives received.                                                 租賃付款減任何已收租賃優
                                                                                              惠。

      2.28 Earnings per share                                                           2.28 每股盈利

      Basic earnings per share is calculated by dividing the profit attributable        每股基本盈利乃按歸屬於本公司權益
      to equity holders of the Company, excluding any costs of servicing                持有人之溢利（不包括普通股以外之
      equity other than ordinary shares by the weighted average number                  任何支付權益成本）除以於財政年度
      of ordinary shares outstanding during the financial year, adjusted                內已發行普通股之加權平均數（已就
      for bonus elements in ordinary shares issued during the year and                  年內已發行之普通股之紅利元素作出
      excluding treasury shares.                                                        調整以及並不包括庫存股份）計算。

      Diluted earnings per share is calculated by adjusting the figures used            每股攤薄盈利乃透過調整用於釐定每
      in the determination of basic earnings per share to take into account             股基本盈利之數字以計及與潛在攤薄
      the post-tax effect of interest and other financing costs associated with         普通股有關之利息及其他融資成本之
      dilutive potential ordinary shares, and the weighted average number of            除所得稅後影響而計算，及假設所有
      additional ordinary shares that would have been outstanding assuming              潛在攤薄普通股換股，則將為額外普
      the conversion of all dilutive potential ordinary shares.                         通股之加權平均數。

      as a liability in the Group’s and Company’s financial statements                  公司股東或董事（如適用）批准期間在
      in the period in which the dividend is approved by the Company’s                  本集團及本公司的財務報表中確認為
      shareholders or directors, when appropriate.                                      負債。

3.   FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                         3.   財務風險管理

     3.1    Financial risk factors                                                          3.1    財務風險因素

     The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks: market               本集團的活動承受著多種的財務風
     risk (including foreign exchange risk, price risk and cash flow and                    險 ： 市 場 風 險（包 括 外 匯 風 險 、 價
     fair value interest rate risk), credit risk and liquidity risk. The Group’s            格風險及現金流量及公平值利率風
     overall risk management program focuses on the unpredictability of                     險）、信用風險及流動性風險。本集

     3.2   Capital risk management                                                      3.2   資本風險管理

     The Group’s objectives for managing capital are to safeguard the                   本集團的資本管理目標是保障本集團
     Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to provide                 能繼續經營，以為股東提供回報和為
     returns for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders and to                其他利益關係者提供利益，同時維持
     maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.               最佳的資本結構以減低資本成本。

     In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may                為了維持或調整資本結構，本集團可
     adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, return capital to             能會調整支付予股東的股息數額、向
     shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets to reduce debts.                     股東退還資本、發行新股或出售資產
                                                                                        以減低債務。

     Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors capital,                與業內其他公司一樣，本集團利用淨
     which comprises all equity components, on the basis of the net                     權益負債比率監察其資本（包括所有
     gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as total borrowings net of cash            權益部分）。此比率按已扣除現金及
     and bank balances and long-term bank deposits divided by total equity              銀行存款及長期銀行存款的總借款除
     excluding non-controlling interests.                                               以總權益（不含非控制性權益）計算。

     3.3   Fair value estimation (continued)                                              3.3       （續）
                                                                                                公平值估計

     Financial instruments in Level 1                                                     第1層金融工具

     The fair value of financial instruments traded in active markets is                  在活躍市場買賣的金融工具的公平值
     based on quoted market prices at the balance sheet dates. A market is                根據資產負債表日的市場報價列賬。
     regarded as active if quoted prices are readily and regularly available              當報價可即時和定期從證券交易所獲
     from an exchange, and the price represents actual and regularly                      得，而該報價代表按公平交易基準進
     occurring market transactions on an arm’s length basis. The quoted                   行的實際和常規市場交易時，該市場
     market price used for financial assets held by the Group is the current              被視為活躍。本集團持有的金融資產
     bid price. These instruments are included in Level 1.                                的市場報價為當時買方報價。此等工
                                                                                          具包括在第1層。

     Financial instruments in Level 3                                                     第3層金融工具

     If one or more of the significant inputs are not based on observable                 倘一個或多個重大輸入數據並非基於
     market data, the instrument is included in Level 3.                                  可觀察的市場數據，則該工具被納入
                                                                                          第3層。

     The fair value of the unlisted unit in an investment fund is estimated by            投資基金中的非上市單位的公平值乃
     referencing the net asset value of the underlying investment.                        經參考相關投資的公平值而釐定。

      (a)   Share premium                                                           (a)   股份溢價

      Pursuant to the Cayman Companies Law and the Articles of Association          根據開曼群島公司法及公司章程細
      of the Company, share premium of the Company is available for                 則，本公司的股份溢價可供分派予股
      distribution to shareholders subject to a solvency test on the Company        東，惟須受本公司的償債能力測試及
      and the provision of the Articles of Association of the Company.              本公司的公司章程細則條文所限。

      (b)   Capital reserves                                                        (b)   資本儲備

      This represents capital reserves arising from the share swap                  此為於2007年本集團重組時進行股份
      transactions during the Group reorganisation in 2007 and the                  互換交易及重新計量離職後福利責任
      remeasurements of post-employment benefit obligations. For further            產生的資本儲備。有關離職後福利的
      details on the post-employment benefits, see (d) below.                       進一步詳情，請參閱下文（d）。

      (c)   Statutory reserves                                                      (c)   法定儲備

      The PRC laws and regulations require companies registered in                  中國法律法規要求在中國註冊的公司
      the PRC to provide for certain statutory reserves, which are to be            從各自法定財務報表所呈報的淨利潤
      appropriated from the net profit (after offsetting accumulated losses         （抵銷以往年度的累計虧損後）中，於
      from prior years) as reported in their respective statutory financial         分派利潤予權益持有人前，劃撥款項
      statements, before profit distributions to equity holders. All statutory      以提撥若干法定儲備金。所有法定儲
      reserves are created for specific purposes. PRC companies are                 備金均為特別用途而設立。中國公司
      required to appropriate 10% of the statutory net profits to statutory         於分派當年度的除稅後利潤前，須向
      surplus reserves, before distribution of their post-tax profits of the        法定盈餘儲備劃撥淨利潤的10%。當
      current year. A company may discontinue the contribution when the             公司法定盈餘儲備的總和超過其註冊
      aggregate sum of the statutory surplus reserves is more than 50%              資本的50%時，公司可停止劃撥。
      of its registered capital. The statutory surplus reserves shall only be       法定盈餘儲備只可用作彌補公司的虧
      used to make up losses of the companies, to expand the companies’             損、擴大公司的生產營運或增加公司
      production operations, or to increase the capital of the companies. In        的資本。此外，按照董事會的決議
      addition, a company may make further contribution to the discretionary        案，公司可使用其除稅後利潤酌情向
      surplus reserve using its post-tax profits in accordance with resolutions     盈餘儲備作出進一步供款。
      of the Board of Directors.

    (d)   Post-employment benefits – defined benefit plans                              (d)   退休福利 –界定受益計劃

    The Group operates defined benefit pension plans in the Taiwan                      本集團於台灣地區實行一項界定受益
    region. The Group engaged an independent valuer to carry out a                      退休計劃。本集團委聘獨立估值師對
    valuation on post-employment benefits. The present value of the                     僱員退休福利進行估值。界定受益計
    obligations and costs of the defined benefit plans were calculated using            劃的義務與成本現值以預測單位貸計
    the projected unit credit method.                                                   法計算。

    The income statement charge included within operating profit includes               計入經營利潤內的收益表支出包括當
    current service cost, interest cost, past service costs and gains and               期服務成本、利息成本、過往服務成
    losses on settlement and curtailment.                                               本以及結算及縮減的盈虧。

    Remeasurements arising from defined benefit pension plans,                         因界定受益退休計劃而產生的重新計
    comprising actuarial gains and losses, the effect of the asset ceiling             量，包括精算盈虧、資產上限的影響
    (excluding amounts included in net interest on the net defined benefit             （不包括計入界定受益淨負債的淨利
    liability) and the return on plan assets (excluding amounts included               息的金額）以及計劃資產的回報（不包
    in net interest on the net defined benefit liability), are recognised              括計入界定受益淨負債的淨利息的金
    immediately in the consolidated balance sheet with a corresponding                 額），於綜合資產負債表中即時確認，
    debit or credit to capital reserves through other comprehensive                    並於其產生期間透過其他全面收益於
    income in the period in which they occur. Remeasurements are not                   資本儲備內相應扣除或計入。重新計
    reclassified to profit or loss in subsequent periods.                              量於其後期間不會重新分類至損益。

28. INCOME TAX EXPENSE (continued)                                                         （續）
                                                                                   28. 所得稅費用

      According to the new Corporate Income Tax Law of the PRC, starting             根據中國境內新企業所得稅法，由
      from 1 January 2008, a reduced withholding tax rate of 10% will                2008年1月1日起，倘中國境外直屬控
      generally be levied on the immediate holding companies outside the             股公司的中國境內附屬公司宣派2008
      Chinese Mainland when their Chinese Mainland subsidiaries declare              年1月1日後所賺取利潤的股息，直
      dividends out of the profits earned after 1 January 2008. A lower 5%           屬控股公司通常減按10%稅率繳付預
      withholding tax rate may be applied when the immediate holding                 扣稅。根據適用的稅收協定安排及中
      companies of the Chinese Mainland subsidiaries are established in              國境內稅法規定，倘中國境內附屬公
      Singapore or Hong Kong, holding at least 25% interests in the Chinese          司的直屬控股公司在新加坡或香港成
      Mainland subsidiaries and recognised as the beneficial owners of               立，並持有中國境內附屬公司不少於
      the Chinese Mainland subsidiaries according to applicable tax treaty           25%權益，以及符合中國附屬公司受
      arrangements and the PRC tax laws.                                             益所有人身份，則較低的5%預扣稅
                                                                                     稅率可能適用。

      Pillar Two income taxes                                                        支柱二所得稅

      The Group is within the scope of the Pillar Two model rules. The               本集團屬於支柱二模板規則的範圍
      Group has applied the mandatory exception to recognising and                   內。本集團已採用強制性豁免確認及
      disclosing information about deferred tax assets and liabilities arising       披露支柱二所得稅產生的遞延稅項資
      from Pillar Two income taxes, and will account for the Pillar Two              產及負債的資料，並將支柱二所得稅
      income taxes as current tax when incurred. Pillar Two legislation has          在發生時作為當期稅項進行會計處
      been enacted or substantively enacted in certain jurisdictions in which        理。支柱二立法已在本集團運營所在
      the Group operates.                                                            若干司法管轄區頒佈或實質頒佈。

      The Group is still in the process of assessing the potential exposure          本集團仍在根據截至2025年3月31日
      arising from Pillar Two legislation based on the information available         止財政年度的可用資料評估支柱二立
      for the financial year ended 31 March 2025. Based on the assessment            法產生的潛在風險。根據迄今為止進
      carried out so far, the Group does not expect material exposure                行的評估，本集團預期不會面臨重大
      to Pillar Two “top-up” taxes. The Group continues to monitor local             支柱二「補足」稅項風險。本集團繼續
      legislation and other developments in the relevant jurisdictions and to        監察相關司法管轄區的當地法例及其
      evaluate the potential future impact on the financial statements.              他事態發展，並評估對財務報表的潛
                                                                                     在未來影響。
